# Skills · S · part 337

925 entries, sub_skill.skeleton to subagent-driven-development. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sub_skill.skeleton**: - &lt;上一个 sub_skill 的产出，如「sub_skill_01 已产出 blueprint.json」&gt; - &lt;本步要用的 api_code 已在 sub_skill_02 `blue_query_api_docs` 验证过&gt; ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/templates/sub_skill.skeleton) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_01_preflight**: 确定构建模式、目标 Skill、可用的 IdeasWork 连接与工作区，再建立本地草稿和问题台账。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_01_preflight) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_02_discover**: 把目标业务流程拆成可实现的步骤，为每一步核对实际可用的 Skill 或 API。转化模式盘点源 Skill； 沉淀已有成果时优先整理已经采用的调用和文档证据，不重新探索全部能力；直接打样从业务步骤开始。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_02_discover) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_03_design**: 把业务流程设计成符合平台 API-First 原则的方案：明确每步是「Agent 直接调 API」还是「薄脚本」，并拆成有序 sub_skill。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_03_design) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_04_prototype**: 用当前 Context 实际授权的能力验证核心参数和结果形态，留下可复现的请求示例。验证仍以完整 Skill 为最终产出。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_04_prototype) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_05_generate**: 把设计与验证结果落成一份可运行的平台格式 Skill，完成本地静态校验并交付本地草稿目录。远端提交只在用户明确要求时才处理，见 §4。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_05_generate) · ★1)
- **sub_skill_06_gapreport**: 收口**真正影响 Skill 落地**的问题，优先完成可用 Skill，而不是为了产出差异报告寻找问题。先判断问题属于 Skill 自身、平台 Tool 能力还是 Agent Runtime/Framework 能力；只对有必要的问题生成反馈计划，并准确路由。 ([BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin](https://github.com/BlueFoucusAI/ideaswork-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideaswork/skills/ideaswork-builder/sub_skill_06_gapreport) · ★1)
- **suba-acquisition**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when data must be brought in before anything else: extract from APIs, feeds or database endpoints into immutable landing artefacts with manifests and hashes, then profile the landed structure and report drift against the baseline. Moves bytes; never cleans, casts, models or analyses, and writes nowhere but the landing target its packet names. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-acquisition) · ★1)
- **suba-analysis**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when a business question needs evidence: classify it as descriptive or causal, answer it with exactly the matching instrument - exploratory statistics or hypothesis testing, never merged - and return a bounded claim with its method, assumptions, confounders and residual uncertainty. Never told the hoped-for answer; never defines criteria, extracts or models data. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-analysis) · ★1)
- **suba-architect**: Regency subagent of the DV role. Use when DV needs structure before a build: decompose the objective into components with responsibilities, boundaries, interfaces, dependencies and invariants, at the rung of DV's scale ladder the step asks for, and return the smallest sufficient architecture with its structural decisions as ADR drafts. Does not define requirements, write production code or verify. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-architect) · ★1)
- **suba-design**: Regency subagent of the PO role. Use when the PO must specify what an experience permits: flows, every reachable state with its transitions, affordances and recovery, under the project's design language in its .ux/ folder, and a handoff of checkable conditions so nothing is left for the implementer to invent. Specifies intent only; never evaluates usability, writes criteria or tests, implements, or hands anything to DV or QA directly. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-design) · ★1)
- **suba-implementer**: Regency subagent of the DV role. Use when DV delegates the build stage of a framed task: turn an accepted architecture, story or correction into the smallest working change inside the declared span, with the named test seen red then green, and return commit-ready change notes to DV. Does not define requirements, decide architecture, accept its own work or commit. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-implementer) · ★1)
- **suba-modeling**: Regency subagent of the DA role. Use when landed data need durable structure or a binding guarantee: declare the grain, design facts and dimensions with their SCD strategy, define semantic metrics consistent with that grain, and write the data contract - name, trigger, payload, types, optionality, key, granularity - with lineage for every engineered feature. Never extracts, tests hypotheses or defines what a metric means to the business. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-modeling) · ★1)
- **suba-recon**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when QA needs the verification context before judging: map the environment a change lives in - the norm slices that govern it, conventions, contracts, tooling, the test surface - compress recurring knowledge into pointers with provenance, and turn a verified finding into a correction brief in QA's bug-report shape. Read-only; never fixes, redesigns or sets severity. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-recon) · ★1)
- **suba-requirements-analyst**: Regency subagent of the PO role. Use when the PO frames a story: bound the client's intent into a problem domain with explicit scope, surface assumptions and contradictions, and specify verifiable Given/When/Then criteria, invariants and state transitions, each traced to a briefing field, a recorded answer or a norm, with criticality and maximum tolerable severity. Never invents objectives, writes tests or designs systems; new rules leave as norm drafts. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-requirements-analyst) · ★1)
- **suba-research**: Proposal - a Regency subagent of the PO role, drafted by an agent and awaiting the Regent's ratification. Use when the PO needs evidence about users before framing or after a build: a user model and goals traced to their sources, claims about users audited for support, closed questions for the client, and - when a built result is under review - where it failed its users' goals. Works only from the sources the PO's packet holds. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-research) · ★1)
- **suba-scribe**: Regency subagent of the DV role, also called directly through /regency:scribe. Under DV it turns a concept, requirement or development problem into a critically examined, causally structured development artefact by chaining the inference engines inside DV's span. Called directly it writes a text for its caller - a document, spec, essay or chapter - with the same engines and none of DV's authority. One subagent, two jurisdictions. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-scribe) · ★1)
- **suba-strategy**: Proposal - a Regency subagent of the PO role, drafted by an agent and awaiting the Regent's ratification. Use when the PO must decide what is worth building and in what order: the value hypothesis and success metric, scope cuts that keep MINIMUM strictly smaller than GRAND, modules ordered by real dependency, the smallest function rung that satisfies each rule, and the trade-offs between scope, deadline, debt and residual risk. Prepares the decision; the PO decides. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-strategy) · ★1)
- **suba-tester**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when QA needs justified coverage: form a test strategy proportionate to risk - surfaces, techniques, the invariants worth defending, the coherence-matrix cases a change must keep - author the tests it implies as verbatim artefacts, confirm each named test fails when its behaviour is removed, and report as evidence only what actually ran. Does not judge criteria, classify defects, set severity or issue the version verdict. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-tester) · ★1)
- **suba-validator**: Regency subagent of the QA role. Use when evidence exists and needs meaning: judge it against each stated criterion (met, not met, partially met, not verifiable), measure risk when it is not yet known, and classify verified failures against the coherence matrix - regression or new, type, severity input, priority - each keyed by its class. Does not gather evidence, rewrite criteria, write the correction brief or issue the version verdict. ([Trevojan/regency](https://github.com/Trevojan/regency/tree/HEAD/library/subagents/suba-validator) · ★1)
- **subaccount**: Configure sub-account creation with parent ownership, contract deployment, init calls, composable transaction hooks, and lifecycle callbacks with automatic rollback. Load when setting up subAccount config, deploying contracts to new sub-accounts, or handling post-creation side effects. Sub-account creation surface has been stable since 1.7.0; the 1.8.x client improvements (getNearClient, detectNearAccount, session-signal notify) do not change the createSubAccount / checkSubAccountAvailability endpoints. ([elliotBraem/better-near-auth](https://github.com/elliotBraem/better-near-auth/tree/HEAD/skills/subaccount) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **subaccount-migration-orchestrator**: Orchestrate complete Neo subaccount configuration migration to Cloud Foundry. Collects all required inputs upfront, then invokes the subaccount migration skills in sequence: trust migration and roles export. Destinations and keystores migration is deferred to neo-destinations-keystores-migrator (run after all CF apps are deployed). Produces a consolidated migration report. Use when you want a single command to migrate all platform-level configuration from a Neo subaccount to CF. Individual skills can also be invoked standalone for partial migrations. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-migration-orchestrator) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **subaccount-onboarding**: Onboard a new agency client end-to-end — create the sub-account, set pricing economics, wire integrations, copy a template agent, and send the welcome invite. Compresses the 30-minute UI workflow into one command. Requires an agency-tier API key. Triggers on /subaccount-onboarding, onboard a new client, set up a new client, set up a sub-account, create a sub-account, create a sub-account for [name], add a client, new client signup, provision a client, spin up a new client, new agency client, new sub-account for [name], we just signed [client]. ([colliercoder/LeadLockAgentKit](https://github.com/colliercoder/LeadLockAgentKit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/subaccount-onboarding) · ★2)
- **subaccount-roles-export**: Export application roles, groups, and user assignments from a Neo subaccount. Uses the public Neo Authorization Management REST API to fetch all application roles with their user assignments, all groups with their role and user assignments, and saves a structured JSON report to ~/.neo-migration/$SUBACCOUNT/$CF_SUBACCOUNT_GUID/neo-roles.json with migration notes for CF. Invoke when migrating authorization configuration from a Neo subaccount to Cloud Foundry, or when you need to understand what roles and groups are configured. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-roles-export) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **subaccount-roles-import**: Assign Neo application roles and group memberships into a CF subaccount after apps are deployed. Reads ~/.neo-migration/$SUBACCOUNT/$CF_SUBACCOUNT_GUID/neo-roles.json (produced by subaccount-roles-export), resolves live XSUAA appIds from deployed CF apps, adds role-templates from those apps to the role collections created by authentication-xsuaa, and assigns users via BTP CLI. Consent files live under ~/.neo-migration-consents/$SUBACCOUNT/. Generates a manual checklist for unresolved apps, group role links, and the Everyone role. MUST run after all applications are deployed to CF. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-roles-import) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **subaccount-trust-migrator**: Migrate application IdP trust configuration from a Neo subaccount to Cloud Foundry in a single pass. Fetches the SAML trust config from Neo via the Trust Management REST API, classifies each identity provider as IAS or third-party, creates SAML trust in the CF subaccount via the XSUAA apiaccess REST API, and assigns assertion-based group rules via BTP CLI. Data flows entirely in memory — nothing is written to disk and the model never sees the raw trust configuration. Invoke when migrating IdP/trust configuration from Neo to Cloud Foundry as part of subaccount migration. ([SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration](https://github.com/SAP-samples/btp-neo-java-app-migration/tree/HEAD/ai-migration/neo-java-migration-skills/skills/subaccount-trust-migrator) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **subadditive-mutual-f-information**: Subadditivity of mutual f-information via g(x)=x²f″. Use when generalizing MI with f-divergences. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/subadditive-mutual-f-information) · ★4)

- **subagent**: Fan out a one-shot or flat parallel batch of cc-fleet PROVIDER subagents (headless `cc-fleet subagent`) that return a result — DeepSeek / GLM / Kimi / Qwen / MiniMax, or a Codex/Claude subscription. - **subagent**: 通用 Sub-agent 编排技能。用户明确要求委派，或任务中有大量无关上下文需要隔离、复杂工作可由独立工作包并行推进、重要结论的偏差风险值得独立审查时使用；可由其他 Skill 加载或按上述场景隐式触发。先比较主会话直接处理与委派的收益和成本，再决定是否创建子 Agent；简单任务、强依赖和共享状态工作通常直接处理。负责必要的任务分派、上下文隔离、结果回收与复核，不因任务可拆分或出现 Review、验收等词就要求委派。 ([Fly3n/flywheel](https://github.com/Fly3n/flywheel/tree/HEAD/development/subagent) · ★7)
- **subagent**: Internal protocol for sva optimization subagents. Not user-invocable -- read by subagents spawned from /optimize. ([bayeslabs-rsi/Svatah](https://github.com/bayeslabs-rsi/Svatah/tree/HEAD/plugins/sva/skills/subagent) · ★6)

- **subagent**: Decides whether selected delegated work needs agents and designs a strict, product-correct subagent contract with isolated context, bounded tools, authority, deliverables, and tests. Use when the user asks to create, configure, delegate to, or troubleshoot Claude or Codex subagents, parallel agents, custom agents, or managed-agent workflows; not for open mechanism comparisons. - **subagent**: Run issues inline as subagents directly from a PM thread — any tier, except issues too big for a subagent. Assesses subagent fit, spawns Phase A/B/C agents, monitors progress, and reports merge readiness. Use to execute selected issues inline instead of in separate coding threads. ([auerbachb/claude-code-config](https://github.com/auerbachb/claude-code-config/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/subagent) · ★5)
- **subagent**: Delegate a bounded research or implementation task to a separate non-interactive pi process to conserve the main agent's context. Use when about to do a deep code dive or a self-contained implementation that can be handed off. Spawns a Sonnet subagent (read-only tools for research, full tools for implementation) with the task prompt and final result persisted as markdown under .pi/tmp/&lt;task_id&gt;/. - **subagent**: Delegate self-contained work to a subagent by default — exploration, planning, review, implementation, or multimodal reading — unless the task is solvable in a line or a single command. Dispatch is non-blocking, keeps the main context lean, and enables parallel work. Model choice: a role's models.suggested are binding when present; models.blacklist fences the agent's autonomous picks; permissions/timeout are main-agent-decided. ([garnetlyx/pi-goal-kit](https://github.com/garnetlyx/pi-goal-kit/tree/HEAD/extensions/subagent) · ★5)
- **subagent**: Multi-subagent framework. Allows the main agent to spawn specialized Claude Code subagents with specific skills. - **subagent**: Claude Code の subagent (Task / Agent tool による子エージェント委譲) を「いつ・どう使うか」を判断し、適切な subagent_type で起動して並列に作業させるための skill。メインの context window を圧迫せず、調査・探索・並列レビュー・独立タスクを子エージェントに任せて高速化するために使う。ユーザーが「subagent で調べて」「並列に調査して」「Explore で探して」「並列でやって」「context 汚したくない」「複数同時に走らせて」「sub agent」「サブエージェント」「Task tool」「Agent tool」「並列レビュー」「3 つ同時に」等を言ったとき、また自分が広範囲な調査・複数の独立タスク・別 context が欲しいタスクを行おうとしているときには必ずこの skill を起動して subagent 起動を検討すること。並列調査・探索・レビューを sequential にやろうとしているときの「気付き skill」としても機能する。 ([yusei531642/vibe-editor](https://github.com/yusei531642/vibe-editor/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/subagent) · ★4)
